FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
INNER JOIN [Customers] AS [c] ON [s].[CustomerID] = [c].[Id]
INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[Id], [m0].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m0].[CreateAt], [m0].[CssSelector], [m0].[CustomerID], [m0].[IsActive], [m0].[LastCheckedAt], [m0].[LastContentHash], [m0].[NotificationChannel], [m0].[URL]
    FROM (
        SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m].[CreateAt], [m].[CssSelector], [m].[CustomerID], [m].[IsActive], [m].[LastCheckedAt], [m].[LastContentHash], [m].[NotificationChannel], [m].[URL], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [m].[CustomerID] ORDER BY [m].[Id]) AS [row]
        FROM [Monitors] AS [m]
        WHERE [m].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit) AND ([m].[LastCheckedAt] IS NULL OR @__8__locals1_now_2 >= DATEADD(minute, CAST(CAST([m].[CheckIntervalMinutes] AS float) AS int), [m].[LastCheckedAt]))
    ) AS [m0]
    WHERE [m0].[row] <= @__limit_3
) AS [m1] ON [c].[Id] = [m1].[CustomerID]
WHERE [s].[EndDate] >= @__now_0 AND [s].[SubscriptionStatus] = 1 AND [s].[PackageType] = @__packageType_1
ORDER BY [s].[PackageType] DESC, [s].[Id], [c].[Id], [a].[Id]

SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m].[CreateAt], [m].[CssSelector], [m].[CustomerID], [m].[IsActive], [m].[LastCheckedAt], [m].[LastContentHash], [m].[NotificationChannel], [m].[URL], [c].[Id], [c].[CreateAt], [c].[IsActive], [a].[Id], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[IsLogin], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[NotificationEmail], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TelegramChatId], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName], [a].[Webhook], [m0].[Id], [m0].[CreateAt], [m0].[ErrorMessage], [m0].[ErrorType], [m0].[IsResolved], [m0].[MonitorId], [m0].[StackTrace]
FROM [Monitors] AS [m]
INNER JOIN [Customers] AS [c] ON [m].[CustomerID] = [c].[Id]
INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]

SELECT [s].[Id], [s].[CreateAt], [s].[CustomerID], [s].[EndDate], [s].[PackageType], [s].[StartDate], [s].[SubscriptionStatus], [c].[Id], [c].[CreateAt], [c].[IsActive], [a].[Id], [m1].[Id], [m1].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m1].[CreateAt], [m1].[CssSelector], [m1].[CustomerID], [m1].[IsActive], [m1].[LastCheckedAt], [m1].[LastContentHash], [m1].[NotificationChannel], [m1].[URL], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[IsLogin], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[NotificationEmail], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TelegramChatId], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName], [a].[Webhook]
FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
INNER JOIN [Customers] AS [c] ON [s].[CustomerID] = [c].[Id]
INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[Id], [m0].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m0].[CreateAt], [m0].[CssSelector], [m0].[CustomerID], [m0].[IsActive], [m0].[LastCheckedAt], [m0].[LastContentHash], [m0].[NotificationChannel], [m0].[URL]
    FROM (
        SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m].[CreateAt], [m].[CssSelector], [m].[CustomerID], [m].[IsActive], [m].[LastCheckedAt], [m].[LastContentHash], [m].[NotificationChannel], [m].[URL], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [m].[CustomerID] ORDER BY [m].[Id]) AS [row]
        FROM [Monitors] AS [m]
        WHERE [m].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit) AND ([m].[LastCheckedAt] IS NULL OR @__8__locals1_now_2 >= DATEADD(minute, CAST(CAST([m].[CheckIntervalMinutes] AS float) AS int), [m].[LastCheckedAt]))
    ) AS [m0]
    WHERE [m0].[row] <= @__limit_3
) AS [m1] ON [c].[Id] = [m1].[CustomerID]
WHERE [s].[EndDate] >= @__now_0 AND [s].[SubscriptionStatus] = 1 AND [s].[PackageType] = @__packageType_1

SELECT [s].[Id], [s].[CreateAt], [s].[CustomerID], [s].[EndDate], [s].[PackageType], [s].[StartDate], [s].[SubscriptionStatus], [c].[Id], [c].[CreateAt], [c].[IsActive], [a].[Id], [m1].[Id], [m1].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m1].[CreateAt], [m1].[CssSelector], [m1].[CustomerID], [m1].[IsActive], [m1].[LastCheckedAt], [m1].[LastContentHash], [m1].[NotificationChannel], [m1].[URL], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[IsLogin], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[NotificationEmail], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TelegramChatId], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName], [a].[Webhook]
FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
INNER JOIN [Customers] AS [c] ON [s].[CustomerID] = [c].[Id]
INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[Id], [m0].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m0].[CreateAt], [m0].[CssSelector], [m0].[CustomerID], [m0].[IsActive], [m0].[LastCheckedAt], [m0].[LastContentHash], [m0].[NotificationChannel], [m0].[URL]
    FROM (
        SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[CheckIntervalMinutes], [m].[CreateAt], [m].[CssSelector], [m].[CustomerID], [m].[IsActive], [m].[LastCheckedAt], [m].[LastContentHash], [m].[NotificationChannel], [m].[URL], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [m].[CustomerID] ORDER BY [m].[Id]) AS [row]
        FROM [Monitors] AS [m]
